       ---
       COMPREHENSIVE WORKOUT BUILDER APPLICATION ANALYSIS REPORT

       1. APPLICATION OVERVIEW

       Location: /Users/mymac/Downloads/REPLIT

       Application Type: Full-stack web application (Weightlifting Team Management System)

       Primary Purpose: Professional weightlifting training program management platform for coaches and athletes

       Codebase Size:
       - 85 TypeScript/TSX files
       - ~7,891 lines of code (core files)
       - No node_modules installed (dependencies not installed)

       ---
       2. TECHNOLOGY STACK

       Frontend:
       - React 18.3.1 with TypeScript
       - Wouter (routing)
       - TanStack Query (data fetching)
       - Shadcn UI components (Radix UI primitives)
       - TailwindCSS 3.4.17 for styling
       - Framer Motion for animations
       - Vite 5.4.20 as build tool

       Backend:
       - Node.js with Express.js 4.21.2
       - TypeScript 5.6.3
       - Drizzle ORM 0.39.1 (PostgreSQL)
       - NeonDB (@neondatabase/serverless) for database
       - Replit Auth for authentication (OAuth + email/password)
       - Session storage with connect-pg-simple

       Development Tools:
       - tsx for TypeScript execution
       - ESBuild for production builds
       - Drizzle Kit for database migrations
       - Replit-specific Vite plugins

       ---
       3. APPLICATION STRUCTURE

       REPLIT/
       ├── client/
       │   ├── src/
       │   │   ├── components/       # UI components (Shadcn)
       │   │   ├── contexts/         # React contexts (AppContext)

       │   │   ├── lib/              # Utilities, queryClient
       │   │   ├── pages/            # 12 main pages

       │   │   ├── main.tsx          # Entry point

       │   ├── storage.ts            # Database operations
       │   ├── errors.ts             # Error handling
       │   ├── replitAuth.ts         # Authentication setup

       │   └── index.ts              # Express server setup

       ├── tsconfig.json             # TypeScript config
       ├── vite.config.ts            # Vite configuration
       └── .replit                   # Replit deployment config

       ---
       4. KEY FEATURES

       For Coaches:
       - Organization and team management
       - Training program builder (hierarchical: Programs → Weeks → Days → Exercises)
       - Program assignment to athletes
       - Team member management
       - Join request approval/rejection
       - Progress tracking and analytics
       - Exercise library (193 exercises + custom exercises)

       For Athletes:
       - Team search and join requests
       - Workout logging (sets, reps, RPE)
       - Progress visualization
       - Program viewing
       - Rest timer and plate calculator tools
       - Mobile-optimized workout interface

       System Features:
       - Role-based access control (Admin, Head Coach, Assistant Coach, Athlete)
       - Real-time messaging
       - Session management
       - Habit tracking (water, weight, measurements)
       - Dark mode theme (Material Design 3)

       ---
       5. DATABASE SCHEMA

       Core Tables:
       - users (varchar ID from Replit Auth)
       - organizations (UUIDs)
       - teams (UUIDs)
       - team_members (junction table)
       - team_join_requests (with status enum)
       - exercises (193 pre-loaded + custom)
       - programs → program_weeks → program_days → program_exercises
       - program_assignments
       - workout_sessions → exercise_logs → set_logs
       - messages
       - habit_trackers → habit_entries
       - sessions (for authentication)

       Relationships:
       - Cascade deletes configured throughout
       - Foreign key constraints
       - Unique constraints on critical fields

       ---
       6. CRITICAL ERRORS & ISSUES

       CRITICAL (Must Fix):

       1. Missing Dependencies ⚠️
         - Location: Root directory
         - Issue: node_modules folder does not exist
         - Impact: Application cannot run
         - Fix Required: Run npm install
       2. N+1 Query Problem 🔴
         - Location: server/storage.ts - getProgramWeeks() method
         - Issue: Makes 73+ database queries for a single 12-week program (1 + N weeks + M days per week)
         - Impact: Severe performance degradation, slow page loads
         - Evidence from docs: "1 + 12 + (12 × 5) = 73 database queries for one program!"
       3. No Atomic Program Creation 🔴
         - Location: server/routes.ts - Program creation endpoints
         - Issue: Creating a complete program requires multiple separate API calls with no transaction wrapping
         - Impact: Partial program creation if any step fails, orphaned data
         - Fix Required: Implement POST /api/programs/complete with transaction wrapping
       4. Seed Data Schema Mismatch 🔴
         - Location: server/seedAllExercises.ts
         - Issue: Uses fields category and muscleGroup which don't match actual schema fields
         - Expected: Schema has category, muscleGroup, equipment fields
         - Actual: Seed data structure may not align properly
       5. SQL Injection Risk 🔴
         - Location: server/storage.ts - searchTeams() method
         - Issue: Potentially unsafe SQL template usage
         - Impact: Security vulnerability
       6. Memory Leak in Rate Limiter 🔴
         - Location: server/middleware/sanitization.ts
         - Issue: Rate limiter Map never cleans up old entries
         - Impact: Memory grows unbounded over time

       HIGH PRIORITY:

       7. Missing Error Boundary in Production ⚠️
         - Location: client/src/App.tsx
         - Status: FIXED (Error boundary is present in code)
         - Note: The error docs show this was fixed - ErrorBoundary with ErrorFallback component exists
       8. Incomplete User Profile Handling ⚠️
         - Location: server/replitAuth.ts
         - Issue: Doesn't handle incomplete user profiles during OAuth authentication
         - Impact: Potential crashes during login
       9. No Validation on Program Structure ⚠️
         - Multiple locations in server/routes.ts
         - Issues:
             - No max validation on day numbers (can create day 999)
           - No validation on exercise order (can create duplicate orders)
           - Can create negative sets
           - No week number uniqueness check
           - No day number uniqueness check within weeks
       10. Missing Cascade Delete Logic ⚠️
         - Location: Throughout server/storage.ts
         - Issue: While schema has cascade deletes, some complex deletions may leave orphaned data
         - Impact: Database bloat, referential integrity issues
       11. No Transaction Wrapping ⚠️
         - Location: Multiple database operations in server/storage.ts
         - Issue: Related queries not wrapped in transactions
         - Impact: Race conditions, data inconsistency

     // Centralized logging system
     // Provides structured logging with different log levels
     // Can be easily extended to integrate with external logging services

     export enum LogLevel {
       DEBUG = 0,
       INFO = 1,
       WARN = 2,
       ERROR = 3,
     }

     class Logger {
       private minLevel: LogLevel;
       private isDevelopment: boolean;

       constructor() {
         this.isDevelopment = process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production';
         this.minLevel = this.isDevelopment ? LogLevel.DEBUG : LogLevel.INFO;
       }

       private formatMessage(level: string, message: string, meta?: any): string {
         const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
         const metaStr = meta ? ` ${JSON.stringify(meta)}` : '';
         return `[${timestamp}] [${level}] ${message}${metaStr}`;
       }

       private shouldLog(level: LogLevel): boolean {
         return level >= this.minLevel;
       }

       debug(message: string, meta?: any): void {
         if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.DEBUG)) {
           console.log(this.formatMessage('DEBUG', message, meta));
         }
       }

       info(message: string, meta?: any): void {
         if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.INFO)) {
           console.log(this.formatMessage('INFO', message, meta));
         }
       }

       warn(message: string, meta?: any): void {
         if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.WARN)) {
           console.warn(this.formatMessage('WARN', message, meta));
         }
       }

       error(message: string, error?: Error | any, meta?: any): void {
         if (this.shouldLog(LogLevel.ERROR)) {
           const errorMeta = error instanceof Error
             ? { message: error.message, stack: error.stack, ...meta }
             : { error, ...meta };
           console.error(this.formatMessage('ERROR', message, errorMeta));
         }
       }

       // Helper methods for common patterns
       logRequest(method: string, path: string, statusCode: number, duration: number): void {
         this.info(`${method} ${path} ${statusCode}`, { duration: `${duration}ms` });
       }

       logDatabaseQuery(query: string, duration?: number): void {
         if (this.isDevelopment) {
           this.debug('Database query', { query, duration: duration ? `${duration}ms` : undefined });
         }
       }

       logAuth(userId: string, action: string, success: boolean): void {
         this.info(`Auth: ${action}`, { userId, success });
       }

       logSeed(message: string, stats?: { inserted?: number; skipped?: number; total?: number }): void {
         this.info(`Seed: ${message}`, stats);
       }
     }

     // Export singleton instance
     export const logger = new Logger();

     - **No action required**

     - **Location**: `server/storage.ts:307-322`
     - **Status**: Drizzle ORM automatically uses parameterized queries

     - **No action required**

     - **Location**: `server/middleware/sanitization.ts:105-112`

     - **No action required**

     ## ✅ CRITICAL ISSUES FIXED

     ### 1. ✅ **FIXED** - Unsafe localStorage Access (Onboarding Page)
     **File:** `client/src/pages/onboarding.tsx`
     **Lines:** 75-147
     **Severity:** CRITICAL

     **Problem:**
     ```typescript
     // BEFORE - UNSAFE
     const org = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('currentOrganization') || '{}');
     if (org.id) { // Could be undefined!
       createTeamMutation.mutate({ organizationId: org.id, name: teamName });
     }
     ```

     **Solution:**
     ```typescript
     // AFTER - SAFE
     if (!currentOrganization?.id) {
       toast({ title: "Error", description: "No organization found..." });
       setStep('org');
       return;
     }
     createTeamMutation.mutate({
       organizationId: currentOrganization.id,
       name: trimmedName
     });
     ```

     **Additional Improvements:**
     - ✅ Added proper form validation (min 3 chars, max 100 chars)
     - ✅ Using currentOrganization from AppContext instead of localStorage
     - ✅ Added comprehensive error handling
     - ✅ Better user feedback with toast messages

     ### 4. ✅ **FIXED** - Missing Email Validation
     **File:** `client/src/pages/register.tsx`
     **Lines:** 52-87
     **Severity:** HIGH

     **Problem:**
     ```typescript
     // BEFORE - NO EMAIL VALIDATION
     if (!firstName.trim() || !lastName.trim() || !email.trim()) {
       return; // Only checks if empty
     }
     ```

     **Solution:**
     ```typescript
     // AFTER - COMPREHENSIVE VALIDATION
     // 1. Check required fields
     if (!firstName.trim() || !lastName.trim() || !email.trim()) { ... }

     // 2. Validate email format
     const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
     if (!emailRegex.test(email)) {
       toast({ title: "Invalid Email", description: "Please enter a valid email..." });
       return;
     }

     // 3. Validate name lengths
     if (firstName.trim().length < 2 || lastName.trim().length < 2) {
       toast({ title: "Validation Error", description: "Names must be at least 2 chars" });
       return;
     }
     ```

     **Validation Added:**
     - ✅ Email format validation (regex)
     - ✅ Minimum name length (2 chars)
     - ✅ Trim whitespace before validation
     - ✅ User-friendly error messages

     ## 🚀 PERFORMANCE OPTIMIZATIONS ADDED

     ### 5. ✅ **FIXED** - Expensive Computations Without Memoization
     **Files:** `client/src/pages/programs.tsx`, `client/src/pages/athletes.tsx`
     **Severity:** MEDIUM

     #### Programs Page - allDays Computation
     **Problem:**
     ```typescript
     // BEFORE - RUNS ON EVERY RENDER
     const allDays = programWeeks?.flatMap((week) =>
       (week.days || []).map((day) => ({ ...day, weekNumber: week.weekNumber }))
     ) || [];
     ```

     **Solution:**
     ```typescript
     // AFTER - MEMOIZED
     const allDays = useMemo(() => {
       return programWeeks?.flatMap((week) =>
         (week.days || []).map((day) => ({ ...day, weekNumber: week.weekNumber }))
       ) || [];
     }, [programWeeks]);
     ```

     #### Athletes Page - Filtered Members
     **Problem:**
     ```typescript
     // BEFORE - FILTERS ON EVERY RENDER
     const filteredMembers = teamMembers?.filter((member) =>
       member.user && member.user.firstName?.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
     );
     ```

     **Solution:**
     ```typescript
     // AFTER - MEMOIZED + OPTIMIZED
     const filteredMembers = useMemo(() => {
       if (!teamMembers) return undefined;
       const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase(); // Only lowercase once
       return teamMembers.filter((member) =>
         member.user && member.user.firstName?.toLowerCase().includes(query)
       );
     }, [teamMembers, searchQuery]);
     ```

     **Performance Gains:**
     - ✅ No unnecessary re-computations
     - ✅ Only re-runs when dependencies change
     - ✅ Optimized toLowerCase() calls
     - ✅ Better React rendering performance

           throw new Error(`Missing environment variables: ${missing.join(', ')}`);

       }

       ✓ Team Join Requests (Create, Read, Approve, Reject, Delete)

       Missing UPDATE Operations:
       - ❌ Program Weeks (no PATCH endpoint)
       - ❌ Exercise Logs (no update after creation)
       - ❌ Set Logs (no update after creation)
       - ❌ Team Join Requests (no update message/details)
       - ❌ Teams (no update name/description)

       Missing DELETE Operations:
       - ❌ Program Weeks (cannot delete individual weeks)
       - ❌ Program Days (cannot delete individual days)
       - ❌ Exercise Logs (no deletion endpoint)
       - ❌ Set Logs (no deletion endpoint)

       Recommendations:
       1. Add missing UPDATE endpoints for data correction
       2. Add DELETE endpoints for data management
       3. Implement soft delete for audit purposes
       4. Document API completeness status

       ---
       7.2 Missing Endpoints

       Severity: MEDIUM

       Notable Missing Functionality:
       1. Bulk Operations:
         - No bulk program assignment
         - No bulk team member addition
         - No bulk exercise import
       2. Search/Filter:
         - Team search exists ✓
         - No exercise search by category/muscle group
         - No program search/filter
         - No athlete search
       3. Analytics:
         - No progress tracking endpoints
         - No 1RM calculation endpoint
         - No volume tracking endpoint
         - No workout statistics endpoint
       4. Habit Tracking:
         - Schema exists but NO API endpoints
         - No habit tracker CRUD
         - No habit entry logging

       Recommendations:
       1. Prioritize analytics endpoints (core feature per replit.md)
       2. Add search/filter capabilities
       3. Either implement habit tracking or remove from schema
       4. Add bulk operation endpoints for efficiency

       ---
       7.3 API Versioning

       Severity: LOW

       Current State:
       - No API versioning strategy
       - All endpoints at /api/*
       - No version in URL or headers

       Risks:
       - Breaking changes will affect all clients
       - No backward compatibility path
       - Difficult to evolve API

       Recommendations:
       1. Implement versioning strategy:
         - URL-based: /api/v1/*
         - Header-based: Accept: application/vnd.app.v1+json
       2. Document versioning policy
       3. Plan for V2 with breaking changes

       ---
       8. CODE QUALITY ISSUES

       8.1 Console.log Usage

       Severity: LOW

       Finding:
       - 69 occurrences of console.log/error/warn/debug across 7 server files
       - Some are legitimate (logger.ts uses console for output)
       - Others should use structured logging

       Files with console usage:
       - server/errors.ts: 1
       - server/logger.ts: 4 (legitimate)
       - server/vite.ts: 1
       - server/seed.ts: 5
       - server/replitAuth.ts: 1
       - server/middleware/authorization.ts: 7
       - server/routes.ts: 50

       Recommendations:
       1. Replace all console.* with structured logger
       2. Add log levels (debug, info, warn, error)
       3. Add request correlation IDs
       4. Consider logging to external service (production)

       ---
       8.2 Error Handling Consistency

       Severity: MEDIUM

       Mixed Error Handling Patterns:
       - Custom error classes defined in errors.ts (good)
       - Centralized error handler exists (good)
       - But many routes use inline error handling with try/catch and res.status().json()
       - Not all routes use the custom error classes
       - Inconsistent error response format

       Recommendations:
       1. Use custom error classes consistently
       2. Let centralized error handler format responses
       3. Remove redundant try/catch blocks
       4. Standardize error response format:
       { error: string, details?: string[], code?: string }

       ---
       9. PERFORMANCE CONCERNS

       9.1 N+1 Query Potential

       Severity: MEDIUM

       Potential N+1 Queries Found:
       - /api/organizations/:orgId/teams - Fetches members for each team in a loop (line 215-222)
       - /api/programs/:programId/assignments - Loops through assignments checking athlete teams (line 995-1003)
       - Multiple authorization checks fetch organization/team data repeatedly

       Optimizations Done:
       ✓ getProgramWeeks() optimized with JOINs (73 queries → 1)

       Recommendations:
       1. Use JOINs or batch loading for team members
       2. Implement DataLoader pattern for authorization checks
       3. Add query performance monitoring
       4. Consider caching frequently accessed data

       ---
       9.2 Missing Caching Strategy

       Severity: MEDIUM

       Current State:
       - OIDC config is memoized (good)
       - No other caching implemented
       - Every request fetches user context from database
       - Exercise library fetched on every request

       Recommendations:
       1. Implement Redis caching for:
         - User context (after auth)
         - Exercise library (global exercises)
         - Organization/team metadata
       2. Add cache invalidation strategy
       3. Use cache headers for static assets
       4. Consider CDN for attached_assets

     # Weightlifting Team Management Platform

     A comprehensive workout programming platform for weightlifting coaches and athletes. Create programs, track workouts, manage teams, and monitor athlete progress.

     ## 🏋️ Features

     ### For Coaches
     - **Program Builder**: Create structured training programs with weeks, days, and exercises
     - **Team Management**: Organize athletes into teams and manage memberships
     - **Athlete Tracking**: Monitor athlete progress and workout completion
     - **Exercise Library**: Access 193+ pre-loaded weightlifting exercises or create custom ones
     - **Program Assignment**: Assign training programs to individual athletes
     - **Join Request Management**: Approve or reject athlete team join requests

     ### For Athletes
     - **Workout Logging**: Log sets, reps, weight, and RPE for each exercise
     - **Progress Tracking**: View personal records and training history
     - **Team Search**: Find and join teams
     - **Mobile-Optimized**: Responsive workout interface for gym use
     - **Rest Timer & Plate Calculator**: Built-in training tools

     ## 🚀 Quick Start

     ### Prerequisites

     - **Node.js** 20+ (LTS recommended)
     - **PostgreSQL** 16+ (or NeonDB account)
     - **npm** or **pnpm**

     ### Installation

     1. **Clone the repository**
        ```bash
        git clone <repository-url>
        cd REPLIT
        ```

     2. **Install dependencies**
        ```bash
        npm install
        ```

     3. **Set up environment variables**
        ```bash
        cp .env.example .env
        ```

        Edit `.env` and add your configuration:
        ```env
        DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:password@host:port/database
        SESSION_SECRET=your-secret-key-min-32-characters
        REPL_ID=your-replit-id
        NODE_ENV=development
        PORT=5000
        ```

     4. **Initialize the database**
        ```bash
        npm run db:push
        ```

     5. **Seed the exercise library** (optional but recommended)
        ```bash
        npm run seed
        ```

     6. **Start the development server**
        ```bash
        npm run dev
        ```

        The app will be available at `http://localhost:5000`

     ## 📦 Project Structure

     ```
     REPLIT/
     ├── client/              # React frontend
     │   ├── src/
     │   │   ├── components/  # UI components (Shadcn)
     │   │   ├── contexts/    # React contexts
     │   │   ├── hooks/       # Custom hooks
     │   │   ├── lib/         # Utilities
     │   │   └── pages/       # Application pages
     │   └── index.html
     ├── server/              # Express backend
     │   ├── middleware/      # Auth & sanitization
     │   ├── db.ts           # Database connection
     │   ├── routes.ts       # API routes
     │   ├── storage.ts      # Database operations
     │   ├── logger.ts       # Logging system
     │   └── index.ts        # Server entry point
     ├── shared/
     │   └── schema.ts       # Database schema (Drizzle ORM)
     ├── migrations/         # Database migrations
     └── package.json
     ```

     ## 🛠 Technology Stack

     ### Frontend
     - **React 18** with TypeScript
     - **TailwindCSS** for styling
     - **Shadcn UI** components (Radix UI)
     - **TanStack Query** for data fetching
     - **Wouter** for routing
     - **Vite** as build tool

     ### Backend
     - **Node.js** with Express
     - **TypeScript**
     - **Drizzle ORM** with PostgreSQL
     - **NeonDB** for serverless PostgreSQL
     - **Replit Auth** for authentication
     - **Zod** for validation

     ## 📝 Available Scripts

     ```bash
     # Development
     npm run dev              # Start development server (full stack)
     npm run dev:client       # Start client only (Vite)
     npm run dev:server       # Start server only

     # Building
     npm run build            # Build for production
     npm run check            # Type check without emitting

     # Database
     npm run db:push          # Push schema changes to database
     npm run db:generate      # Generate migration files (recommended)
     npm run db:migrate       # Run pending migrations

     # Seeding
     npm run seed             # Seed exercise library (193 exercises)

     # Production
     npm start                # Start production server
     ```

     ## 🗄️ Database Schema

     The application uses PostgreSQL with Drizzle ORM. Key tables include:

     - **users**: User accounts and profiles
     - **organizations**: Coaching businesses
     - **teams**: Groups of athletes within organizations
     - **exercises**: Exercise library (global + custom)
     - **programs**: Training programs with hierarchical structure
       - program_weeks
       - program_days
       - program_exercises
     - **workout_sessions**: Athlete workout logging
       - exercise_logs
       - set_logs
     - **messages**: Coach-athlete communication (coming soon)

     See `shared/schema.ts` for complete schema definition.

     ## 🔐 Authentication

     The platform uses **Replit Auth** which supports:
     - OAuth authentication (GitHub, Google)
     - Email/password authentication
     - Session-based authentication with PostgreSQL storage

     ## 🚢 Deployment

     ### Deploying to Replit

     1. **Set environment variables** in Replit Secrets:
        - `DATABASE_URL`: Your NeonDB connection string
        - `SESSION_SECRET`: Random 32+ character string
        - `REPL_ID`: Provided by Replit
        - `NODE_ENV`: Set to `production`

     2. **Run migrations**:
        ```bash
        npm run db:push
        ```

     3. **Seed exercises** (first time only):
        ```bash
        npm run seed
        ```

     4. **Deploy**: Replit will automatically build and deploy

     ### Deploying Elsewhere

     1. Build the application:
        ```bash
        npm run build
        ```

     2. Set environment variables on your hosting platform

     3. Run database migrations:
        ```bash
        npm run db:push
        ```

     4. Start the production server:
        ```bash
        npm start
        ```

     ## 🧪 Testing

     **Note**: Testing infrastructure is not yet implemented.

     Planned testing stack:
     - Unit tests: Vitest
     - E2E tests: Playwright
     - Coverage: 70%+ target

     ## 📚 API Documentation

     ### Authentication Endpoints

     - `GET /api/auth/user` - Get current user
     - `POST /api/auth/register` - Complete registration
     - `GET /api/login` - Initiate OAuth login
     - `GET /api/logout` - Logout

     ### Organizations

     - `POST /api/organizations` - Create organization
     - `GET /api/organizations/my` - Get user's organizations
     - `GET /api/organizations/:id` - Get organization by ID
     - `PATCH /api/organizations/:id` - Update organization
     - `DELETE /api/organizations/:id` - Delete organization

     ### Teams

     - `POST /api/teams` - Create team
     - `GET /api/teams/search?q=query` - Search teams
     - `GET /api/organizations/:orgId/teams` - Get org teams
     - `DELETE /api/teams/:id` - Delete team

     ### Programs

     - `POST /api/programs` - Create program
     - `GET /api/organizations/:orgId/programs` - Get org programs
     - `GET /api/programs/:id` - Get program details
     - `PATCH /api/programs/:id` - Update program
     - `DELETE /api/programs/:id` - Delete program
     - `POST /api/programs/complete` - Create complete program with weeks/days/exercises

     For complete API documentation, see the inline comments in `server/routes.ts`.

     # Database Configuration
     # Use the same variable name as drizzle.config.ts expects
     DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:password@host:port/database
     # Alternative: For NeonDB serverless
     # DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:password@host.neon.tech/database?sslmode=require

     # Session Secret (REQUIRED)
     # Generate a random string of at least 32 characters
     # Example: openssl rand -base64 32
     SESSION_SECRET=your-secret-key-minimum-32-characters-long

     # Replit Configuration (REQUIRED for Replit deployment)
     REPL_ID=your-replit-id
     ISSUER_URL=https://replit.com/oidc

     # Application Configuration
     NODE_ENV=development
     # PORT is set by Replit in production, defaults to 5000 in development
     PORT=5000

     # Optional: Logging Configuration
     LOG_LEVEL=info


        ```bash
        npm run db:push
        npm run seed
        ```

     4. **Deploy to Replit**
        - Upload REPLIT folder
        - Set environment variables in Secrets
        - Deploy

     1. **Generate Migration** (if schema changed)
        ```bash
        npm run db:generate
        ```

     2. **Run Migration**
        ```bash
        npm run db:migrate
        ```

     3. **Deploy**
        - Push changes
        - Replit auto-deploys

     # Legal Compliance - ACTION REQUIRED

     ## 🚨 CRITICAL: Legal Documents Required Before Production

     Your application **CANNOT be deployed to production** without these legal documents. Failure to include them exposes you to:
     - **GDPR fines**: Up to €20 million or 4% of annual revenue
     - **CCPA fines**: Up to $7,500 per violation
     - **Lawsuits**: User legal action
     - **App Store Rejection**: If mobile apps planned

     ---

     ## Required Legal Documents

     ### 1. Privacy Policy (CRITICAL)

     Create a file: `client/src/pages/privacy-policy.tsx`

     **Must Include:**
     - What data you collect (name, email, workout logs, etc.)
     - How you use the data
     - How long you store data
     - User rights (access, deletion, export)
     - Cookie usage
     - Third-party services (NeonDB, Replit Auth, Google Fonts)
     - Contact information for privacy inquiries
     - GDPR compliance statement
     - CCPA compliance statement (if applicable)
     - Date of last update

     **Template Services:**
     - https://www.termsfeed.com/privacy-policy-generator/
     - https://www.freeprivacypolicy.com/
     - Consult with a lawyer for custom policy

     ### 2. Terms of Service (CRITICAL)

     Create a file: `client/src/pages/terms-of-service.tsx`

     **Must Include:**
     - User agreement to terms
     - Acceptable use policy
     - User responsibilities
     - Intellectual property rights
     - Limitation of liability
     - Dispute resolution
     - Termination conditions
     - Governing law
     - Contact information
     - Date of last update

     **Template Services:**
     - https://www.termsfeed.com/terms-conditions-generator/
     - https://www.termsandconditionsgenerator.com/
     - Consult with a lawyer for custom terms

     ### 3. Cookie Consent Banner (CRITICAL)

     **Implementation Required:**

     ```typescript
     // Install package:
     npm install react-cookie-consent

     // Add to App.tsx:
     import CookieConsent from "react-cookie-consent";

     <CookieConsent
       location="bottom"
       buttonText="I understand"
       cookieName="workout-builder-consent"
       style={{ background: "#2B373B" }}
       buttonStyle={{ color: "#4e503b", fontSize: "13px" }}
       expires={150}
     >
       This website uses cookies to enhance the user experience.{" "}
       <a href="/privacy-policy" style={{ color: "#fff" }}>
         Learn more
       </a>
     </CookieConsent>
     ```

     ---

     ## Required API Endpoints (GDPR Compliance)

     ### 1. Data Export Endpoint

     ```typescript
     // Add to server/routes.ts:
     app.get('/api/users/me/export', isAuthenticated, async (req: AuthRequest, res) => {
       try {
         const userId = req.currentUser!.id;

         // Gather all user data
         const userData = {
           profile: await storage.getUser(userId),
           workoutSessions: await storage.getUserWorkoutSessions(userId),
           programs: await storage.getUserPrograms(userId),
           teams: await storage.getUserTeams(userId),
           messages: await storage.getUserMessages(userId),
           // Add other user data...
         };

         res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
         res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="my-data.json"');
         res.json(userData);
       } catch (error) {
         logger.error("Error exporting user data", error);
         res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to export data" });
       }
     });
     ```

     ### 2. Account Deletion Endpoint

     ```typescript
     // Add to server/routes.ts:
     app.delete('/api/users/me', isAuthenticated, async (req: AuthRequest, res) => {
       try {
         const userId = req.currentUser!.id;

         // Delete all user data (cascade deletes should handle most)
         await storage.deleteUser(userId);

         // Clear session
         req.session.destroy((err) => {
           if (err) logger.error("Session destruction error", err);
         });

         res.json({
           message: "Account deleted successfully",
           success: true
         });
       } catch (error) {
         logger.error("Error deleting user account", error);
         res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to delete account" });
       }
     });
     ```

     ### 3. Add to storage.ts:

     ```typescript
     async deleteUser(userId: string): Promise<void> {
       // Cascade deletes should handle most, but be explicit
       await this.db.delete(users).where(eq(users.id, userId));
       // All related data deleted via CASCADE
     }

     async getUserWorkoutSessions(userId: string) {
       return await this.db.query.workoutSessions.findMany({
         where: eq(workoutSessions.athleteId, userId),
         with: {
           exerciseLogs: {
             with: {
               setLogs: true,
             },
           },
         },
       });
     }

     // Add similar methods for other user data...
     ```

     ## Data Retention Policy

     **Define and implement:**
     - How long you keep user data after account deletion
     - Backup retention periods
     - Anonymization vs deletion
     - Log retention periods

     **Recommended:**
     - Active accounts: Indefinite (while user is active)
     - Deleted accounts: 30-day grace period, then permanent deletion
     - Logs: 90 days
     - Backups: 30 days with user data anonymized

     ```tsx
           variant="outline"

           Download My Data
         </Button>
         <Button
           variant="destructive"
           onClick={handleDeleteAccount}
         >
           Delete My Account
         </Button>
       </CardContent>
     </Card>
     ```
